# Kivro Requirements Catalog

> Generated from `MASTER-SPEC.md` as a traceability layer. `MASTER-SPEC.md` remains authoritative.

## Rules

- Every Master Spec section has at least one `SECTION_COVERAGE` requirement.
- Normative statements are additionally extracted as atomic traceability items.
- If wording here conflicts with the Master Spec, the Master Spec wins.
- Codex must not mark a section-level requirement complete until all behavior in that source section is accounted for.
- `P0` = security/payment correctness; `P1` = required MVP behavior; `P2` = required quality/UX/operations; `P3` is reserved only for explicitly post-MVP work.

## Curated requirements — Local development environment

| ID | Priority | Source | Requirement |
|---|---|---|---|
| DEV-LOCAL-001 | P1 | §551 | Kivro must be fully runnable and meaningfully testable on a supported developer Mac without production cloud infrastructure. |
| DEV-LOCAL-002 | P1 | §552, §554 | Docker Compose local infrastructure must include PostgreSQL, Redis and S3-compatible object storage, with pinned local Redis and a health check. |
| DEV-LOCAL-003 | P1 | §553 | PostgreSQL must use Docker with pinned version, health check and persistent volume; no host PostgreSQL installation required. |
| DEV-LOCAL-004 | P1 | §555 | Use MinIO or equivalent S3-compatible local storage and automatically create required buckets. |
| DEV-LOCAL-005 | P1 | §556 | Kivro Worker must run host-native and independently observable during local development. |
| DEV-LOCAL-006 | P1 | §557 | Full local OpenClaw E2E acceptance must use a real supported OpenClaw runtime; lower test layers may mock adapters. |
| DEV-LOCAL-007 | P0 | §558 | Development buyer jobs must execute in the real sandbox architecture and never directly on host. |
| DEV-LOCAL-008 | P0 | §558 | Sandbox unavailable must fail closed; development must never fall back to host execution. |
| DEV-LOCAL-009 | P1 | §559 | Provide idempotent pnpm dev:setup that starts dependencies, waits for health, migrates, creates buckets and seeds. |
| DEV-LOCAL-010 | P1 | §560 | Provide pnpm dev for cloud-side services and pnpm worker:dev for the host-native Worker. |
| DEV-LOCAL-011 | P1 | §561 | Provide deterministic pnpm dev:demo or equivalent with buyer, seller, credits, Worker bootstrap and representative capabilities. |
| DEV-LOCAL-012 | P0 | §561 | Development-only shortcuts must be impossible to enable accidentally in production. |
| DEV-LOCAL-013 | P1 | §562 | One machine must simulate distinct buyer and seller identities and browser sessions. |
| DEV-LOCAL-014 | P1 | §563 | Local environment must support the complete buyer-to-seller job path through payment, dispatch, Worker, sandbox, OpenClaw, storage, result and ledger. |
| DEV-LOCAL-015 | P1 | §564 | Provide deterministic fake development payment adapter plus Stripe test-mode integration path. |
| DEV-LOCAL-016 | P0 | §564 | Fake payment adapter must be impossible to enable in production. |
| DEV-LOCAL-017 | P1 | §565 | Development financial flows must use the same production ledger/domain logic. |
| DEV-LOCAL-018 | P1 | §566 | Provide safe environment templates and configurable local service endpoints without real secrets. |
| DEV-LOCAL-019 | P0 | §566 | Production must fail closed if development-only flags/adapters are enabled. |
| DEV-LOCAL-020 | P1 | §567 | Expose clear readiness for DB, Redis, storage, API, Worker, OpenClaw and sandbox. |
| DEV-LOCAL-021 | P2 | §568 | Provide documented stop/restart/reset/storage-reset/queue-reset/full-clean workflows. |
| DEV-LOCAL-022 | P1 | §569 | Local logs must correlate one job across all major components. |
| DEV-LOCAL-023 | P1 | §570 | Provide repeatable pnpm test:e2e:local covering the successful local paid/test-paid job flow. |
| DEV-LOCAL-024 | P1 | §570 | Local E2E must cover Worker offline, sandbox unavailable, payment failure, cancellation, invalid output and timeout. |
| DEV-LOCAL-025 | P1 | §571 | Fresh clone must require no manual host installation of PostgreSQL, Redis or S3 infrastructure. |
| DEV-LOCAL-026 | P1 | §571 | Bootstrap/doctor must detect missing OpenClaw/prerequisites and provide actionable instructions. |
| DEV-LOCAL-027 | P0 | §572 | Local development must preserve sandbox, identity, path, SSRF, network and payment security boundaries. |
| DEV-LOCAL-028 | P0 | §572 | Local development must not use production credentials, databases or buckets by default. |
| DEV-LOCAL-029 | P1 | §573 | Local development is complete only when the §573 Definition of Done checklist is satisfied and represented in coverage. |
